Board Committees
Under the Board, there are Board committees, namely Audit Committee, Remuneration Committee, Nomination
Committee and Risk Committee as well as a functional committee, namely ESG Committee. All these committees
perform their distinct roles in accordance with their respective terms of reference.

Nomination Committee
As at 31 December 2021, the Nomination Committee comprised four Directors, including one Executive Director, being
Dr. YIM Fung and three Independent Non-executive Directors, being Dr. SONG Ming (chairman), Mr. TSANG Yiu Keung
and Professor CHAN Ka Keung Ceajer. For the change of its composition during the year and up to the date of this
report, please refer to the section headed under the “Corporate Information”.

Under its terms of reference, the duties of the Nomination Committee include, but not limited to, setting out the
nomination procedures and the process and criteria to select and recommend candidates for directorship, reviewing
the structure and size and composition of the Board, assessing the independence of independent non-executive
directors, and making recommendations to the Board on the selection of individuals nominated for directorships. The
full terms of reference of the Nomination Committee are available on the websites of both the Company and the Stock
Exchange.

The meeting attendance of each member of the Nomination Committee is set out in the paragraph headed “Attendance
Record at Board Meetings, Board Committee Meetings and General Meetings” of this report.

During the year, the Nomination Committee reviewed the structure, size and composition of the Board and opined
that it has the skills, experience and diversity perspective appropriate to the requirements of the Company’s
business and identified and evaluated individuals suitably qualified to become Board members as required and made
recommendations to the Board on the appointment of Non-executive Directors. The Nomination Committee also
reviewed and recommended to the Board in relation to the re-election of the retiring Directors at the AGM held in 2021
as well as assessed the independence of the Independent Non-executive Directors.

Nomination Policy
The Board has adopted the Nomination Policy, with a view to give an adequate consideration on a balanced
composition of the Board, the policy sets out the ways to identify potential candidates, selection criteria, selection
process and appointment or re-appointment procedures with regard to the nomination and appointment of
directors. The selection criteria in assessing the suitability of potential candidates includes, but not limited to, their
professionalism and personal ethics, board experience, expertise, time commitment and measurable objectives set out
in the Board Diversity Policy. The Nomination Committee may use any process it considers appropriate for evaluating
and shortlisting candidates, which may include, personal interviews, background checks, written submission by the
candidates and third party references.
                                                                                           Guotai Junan International | Annual Report 2021   109

                                                                                                            Corporate Government Report




The selection process for each of their appointments shall be evaluated using a substantially similar process and shall
be fair and transparent. The Nomination Committee is responsible for reviewing this policy to ensure the effectiveness
of this policy.

Board Diversity
The Board has adopted the Board Diversity Policy, which is available on the Company’s website. The Company
recognizes the benefits of having a diverse Board to enhance the standards of corporate governance and to realize
a sustainable and balanced development. The Company seeks to achieve board diversity with a number of aspects
includes, but not limited to, gender, age, cultural and educational background, ethnicity, professional experience,
skills, knowledge and length of service. All Board appointments shall be based on meritocracy, and candidates shall
be considered against objective criteria, having due regard for the benefits of diversity of the Board. The Nomination
Committee is responsible for reviewing this policy to ensure the effectiveness of this policy. During the year, the
Nomination Committee has reviewed the Board diversity based on these aspects and considered that it has a balanced
diversity.

Internal Control
The Board acknowledges its responsibility of overseeing the Group’s systems on risk management and internal control
and reviews their effectiveness through meetings of the Risk Committee and the Audit Committee at least twice a year
and at least three times a year respectively. To facilitate the effective and efficient operations and to ensure compliance
with relevant laws and regulations, the Group emphasizes the importance of a sound internal control system which
is also indispensable for mitigating the Group’s risk (including ESG risks) exposures. The Group’s system of internal
control is designed to provide a reasonable, but not absolute, assurance against material misstatement or loss and
to manage and eliminate risks of failure in operational systems and fulfillment of the business objectives. The internal
control system is reviewed on an ongoing basis by the Board in order to make it practical and effective in providing
reasonable assurance in relation to protection of material assets and identification of business risks. The Group is
committed to the identification, monitoring and management of risks associated with business activities and has
implemented a practical and effective control system which includes a defined management structure with limits of
authority, a sound risk management system and periodic review of the Group’s performance by the Audit Committee
and the Risk Committee.

Internal Audit
The Company has an independent internal audit team, which plays an important role in providing objective assurance
to the Board that a sound internal control system is maintained and operated by management. The head of the internal
audit team directly reports to the Audit Committee on audit matters. By adopting a risk-based approach to evaluate
risk level on control environment, the internal audit team makes their audit schedules annually in consultation with, but
independent of, the management, and the audit plan is submitted to the Audit Committee for approval. The head of the
internal audit team shall periodically present audit reports and express opinion to the Audit Committee on the internal
control of the audit units. The annual audit work plan covers major activities and processes of the Group’s operating
business. Moreover, special reviews shall be conducted on request of the Audit Committee or the management.

The Audit Committee has reviewed the adequacy of resources, qualifications and experience of staff of the internal
audit functions, and their training programmes and budget, and considered that the staffing is adequate and the staffs
are competent to carry out their roles and responsibilities, and the performance of the internal audit functions for the
year under review was efficient.

Following the retirement of Ernst & Young as the auditor of the Company at the AGM held on 21 May 2020, KPMG,
Certified Public Accountants, was appointed as the auditor of the Company by the Shareholders at that AGM.

Risk Committee
As at 31 December 2021, the Risk Committee comprised six Directors, including two Executive Directors, being Dr.
YIM Fung and Ms. QI Haiying, and four Independent Non-executive Directors, being Professor CHAN Ka Keung Ceajer
(chairman), Dr. FU Tingmei, Dr. SONG Ming and Mr. TSANG Yiu Keung. For the change of its composition during the
year and up to the date of this report, please refer to the section headed under the “Corporate Information”.

Under its terms of reference, the duties of the Risk Committee include, but not limited to, overseeing management in
design, implementation and monitoring of risk management system and ensuring it has an effective system, advising
the Board on the Group’s overall risk appetite and principles, approving the Group’s risk policies and risk tolerance,
and reviewing major investigation findings on risk management matters as well as significant risk control failings or
weaknesses where identified.

The meeting attendance of each member of the Risk Committee is set out in the paragraph headed “Attendance
Record at Board meetings, Board Committee Meetings and General Meetings” of this report.

During the year, the Risk Committee has discussed with the management to ensure that the management has
performed its duty to have effective risk management system, including the changes, since last review, the nature and
extent of significant risks faced by the Group and its ability to respond to the changes.

Risk Management
The Board has overall responsibility of evaluating and determining the risk appetite and risk tolerance (including ESG
risks) that the Group is willing to take in achieving its strategic objectives, maintaining a sound risk management
system and reviewing its effectiveness on regular basis.

The Risk Committee has approved the amendments of the Group’s Risk Framework by incorporating ESG risks and
related management approach this year, it sets out the risk culture and governance, defining the risk appetite and risk
limit, key risk types and overall risk management process. The Risk Framework serves as an outline of the Group’s
overall risk management system and process, which facilitates the Board to identify and assess the nature and extent
of significant risks (including ESG risks), the Group’s ability to respond to changes in its business and the external
environment, as well as the scope and quality of management’s ongoing risk (including ESG risks) monitoring and
related internal control measures.

The Group’s risk management framework is embodied by the three-tier system, the Risk Committee, which delegated
by the Board, being the first-tier level, whereas the Risk Management Committee, consists of management members
from frontline and internal control functions, being the second-tier level, along with the third-tier level under the Risk
Management Committee, i.e. Risk Management Department, other risk control departments and working groups,
which manage day-to-day specific risks. All significant risks (including ESG risks), material changes and the associated
mitigating actions to enhance the accountability and quality of risk management process shall be reported to the Risk
Committee through the chief risk officer of the Company or the ESG Committee through designated risk management
staff (as the case may be). For details, please refer to the section headed “Risk Management Report” in this annual
report.

Anti-Corruption and Whistleblowing
The Group attaches great importance to integrity and anti-corruption and is committed to the highest standard of
business ethics, with zero tolerance for bribery and corruption activities. The Company’s statement on the anti-bribery,
anti-corruption as well as the audit of its ethical standards are available on the Company’s website.

In December 2021 and March 2022, the Company has upgraded the Professional Integrity Policy and the Anti-bribery
and Anti-corruption Manual (with detailed whistleblowing procedures) from the Compliance Manual as standalone
policies, respectively. The Professional Integrity Policy clarifies the internal control mechanism on corruption and bribery,
sets out specific requirements on integrity, strictly forbids employees to convey or seek illegitimate benefits to others,
and it also clarifies that the Board is responsible for overseeing the effectiveness of the integrity management. The
Anti-Bribery and Anti-Corruption Manual encourages employees and external parties (including clients and suppliers)
to timely report the suspected misconduct or malpractice including bribery and corruption issues to the Company, and
the Company has undertaken to do its best to keep the whistleblower’s identity and related information confidential.
In June 2021, the Company has also revised its Service Procurement Policy, which clearly defines the principles of
openness, transparency, fairness and integrity in its procurement, and lists out control measures on supplier selection,
quotation comparison and contract execution, etc., preventing corruption activities in the procurement process.

Meanwhile, the Company expects our business partners (including contractors, suppliers, service providers,
consultants, representatives, etc.) to comply with the Anti-Bribery and Anti-Corruption Manual of the Company when
performing work or services for the Group. If the service contract exceeds a certain amount, the business partners are
required to sign the Anti-Bribery Undertaking.


Anti-Money Laundering and Counter Financing of Terrorism
The Group pledges to actively prevent money laundering and terrorist financing and comply with applicable laws and
regulations in respect of anti-money laundering and counter-terrorist financing in relevant jurisdictions during the course
of business, such as the Anti-Money Laundering and Counter-Terrorist Financing Ordinance (Cap. 615 of the Laws of
Hong Kong), and continuously to perform various anti-money laundering obligations.

The Group has established the Anti-Money Laundering and Counter Financing of Terrorism Manual and the Customer
Due Diligence Manual, which expressly define money laundering actions, the roles and responsibilities of the three
lines of defense, and requirements on various anti-money laundering efforts, including institutional risk assessment,
client risk assessment, client due diligence, continuous monitoring (e.g. transaction monitoring, regular review, etc.),
suspicious transaction reporting, employee training and record keeping, etc.

The Legal and Compliance Department is responsible for monitoring the execution and implementation of various
anti-money laundering efforts by each department and reporting to the management in a timely manner. Based on the
latest laws and regulations, regulatory requirements and practical situation of the Company, it also amends and refines
relevant rules and further improves the risk management policies in respect of anti-money laundering of the Company.
In addition, the Group also applies advanced monitoring system to the identification of suspicious transactions to
effectively avoid the risk of the Group being used for money laundering and terrorist financing.

Handling and Dissemination of Inside Information
For the purpose of handling and disseminating inside information in accordance with the Listing Rules and the
Securities and Futures Ordinance (Cap. 571 of the Laws of Hong Kong) (the “SFO”), the Group has a policy on
disclosure of inside information with various procedures and measures, including arousing the awareness to preserve
confidentiality of inside information within the Group, sending blackout period and securities dealing restriction
notifications to the directors and relevant employees, and disseminating information to specific persons on a
need-to-know basis. In all cases, great care must be taken to ensure that no inside information will be disclosed to
selected outsiders.

Directors’ Securities Transactions
The Company has adopted the Model Code for Securities Transactions by Directors of Listed Issuers (the “Model
Code”) sets out in Appendix 10 of the Listing Rules as its code of conduct regarding securities transactions by the
directors. On specific enquiry made by the Company, all Directors confirmed that they have complied with the required
standards as set out in the Model Code throughout the year of 2021.


Directors’ Responsibilities on Financial Statements

flow of the Company during the year in a true and fair manner.

The Company Secretary, Ms. FENG Zheng Yao Helen was appointed by the Board. She is an employee of the

Dividend Policy
The Company endeavors to reward Shareholders with stable dividend income while retaining adequate reserves for
its future development. The Company aims to distribute to Shareholders with regular dividends semi-annually with a
certain percentage of Attributable Profit and may also distribute special dividends from special income. The Board
considers that it would be appropriate to target a payout ratio between 30% and 50%, and subject to the economic
conditions as well as the Company’s position. From 2018 to 2021, the annual dividend payments amounted to 51% to
57% of Attributable Profit. The Dividend Policy summary and the dividend statistics are available on the website of the
Company.


Constitutional Documents

meeting held on 30 August 2021 in order to clarify the powers of the Shareholders and the Board and enhance its
corporate governance, as well as to tidy up typographical errors and update the Articles of Association to reflect the
amendments to the Listing Rules since last update. The latest Articles of Association are available on the websites of
the Company and the Stock Exchange.

Communications During COVID-19
The Company values safety and wellbeing of Shareholders and other stakeholders. In 2021, the Company has taken
various measures, such as imposing social distancing restrictions, for face-to-face communications. In light of the
uncertain development of the COVID-19 situation, Shareholders were encouraged to appoint the chairman of the
general meetings as their proxy to vote on the resolutions, instead of attending the general meetings in person, and
most investor meetings were conducted through online platform. In accordance with prevailing guidelines published by
the Hong Kong Government and/or regulatory authorities, the Company will implement additional preventive measures
to ensure the safety of the Shareholders and other stakeholders for the communications and engagements.


Shareholders’ Rights
Subject to the applicable laws and regulations, including but not limited to, the Companies Ordinance (Chapter 622
of the Laws of Hong Kong) (the “CO”), the Listing Rules and the Articles of Association as amended from time to
time, Shareholder(s) may call general meeting, put forward proposals at AGM and direct enquiries to the Board in
accordance with the following procedures:

Convening an Extraordinary General Meeting by Shareholders
Shareholder(s) holding at the date of the deposit of the requisition not less than one-twentieth of the paid up capital of
the Company may request the directors to convene an extraordinary general meeting pursuant to Section 566 to 568
of the CO.

In accordance with sections 566 of the CO, the directors are required to call a general meeting if the Company has
received requests to do so from Shareholder(s) representing at least 5% of the total voting rights of all the Shareholders
having a right to vote at general meeting. Such requests must state the general nature of the business to be dealt with
at the meeting; and may include the text of a resolution that may properly be moved and is intended to be moved at
the meeting. Such requests may be sent to the Company and attention to the Board or the Company Secretary in hard
copy form (by depositing at the registered office of the Company at 27/F., Low Block, Grand Millennium Plaza, 181
Queen’s Road Central, Hong Kong) or in electronic form (by fax: 2509-0030); and must be authenticated by the person
or persons making it. In accordance with Section 567 of the CO, the directors must call a meeting within 21 days after
the date on which they become subject to the requirement under Section 566 of the CO and such meeting must be
held on a date not more than 28 days after the date of the notice convening the meeting.
                                                                              Guotai Junan International | Annual Report 2021   119

                                                                                              Corporate Government Report




Putting Forward Proposals at AGM
To put forward a resolution at AGM, Shareholder(s) is/are requested to follow the requirements and procedures set out
in Section 615 and 616 of the CO.

Section 615 of the CO provides that the Company must give notice of a resolution if it has received requested to do so
from (a) the Shareholder(s) representing at least 2.5% of the total voting rights of all the Shareholders who have a right
to vote on the resolution at the AGM to which the requests relate; or (b) at least 50 Shareholders who have the rights
to vote on the resolution at the AGM to which the requests relate.

Such requests (a) may be sent to the Company and attention to the Board or the Company Secretary in hard copy
form (by depositing at the registered office of the Company at 27/F., Low Block, Grand Millennium Plaza, 181 Queen’s
Road Central, Hong Kong) or in electronic form (by fax: 2509-0030); (b) must identify the resolution of which notice is
to be given; (c) must be authenticated by the person or persons making it; and (d) must be received by the Company
not later than (i) 6 weeks before the AGM to which the requests relate; or (ii) if later, the time at which notice is given of
that meeting. Section 616 of the CO provides that the Company that is required under Section 615 of the CO to give
notice of a resolution must send a copy of it at the Company’s own expense to each Shareholder entitled to receive
notice of the AGM (a) in the same manner as the notice of the meeting; and (b) at the same time as, or as soon as
reasonably practicable after, it gives notice of the meeting.

The procedures for Shareholders to propose a person for election as a director can be found on the website of the
Company at www.gtjai.com.

the securities industry. Dr. YIM is a Senior Economist and holds a doctorate degree in Economics from the Graduate
School of the Chinese Academy of Social Sciences and a bachelor’s degree in Environmental Engineering from

Report of the Directors




Shared-based Compensation Scheme
The Company adopted two equity-settled share-based compensation schemes including the Share Option Scheme
and the Share Award Scheme. Details of which are set out in the paragraphs below.

Share Option Scheme
The Company adopted the Share Option Scheme on 19 June 2010. The Share Option Scheme was valid for a period
of 10 years from the date of adoption and has expired on 19 June 2020. No further share options shall be granted
after it expired, but all granted and outstanding share options remain exercisable for a period of 10 years after the
respective dates of grant, the provisions of the Share Option Scheme thereof remain in full force and effect.

The purpose of the Share Option Scheme was to provide the people working for the interests of the Group with an
opportunity to obtain equity interest in the Company, thus linking their interests of the Group and thereby providing
them with an incentive to work better for the interests of the Group.

The participants of the Share Option Scheme were the directors (whether executive or non-executive and whether
independent or not) and employees of the Group who, in the absolute opinion of the Board, had contributed to the
Company or the Group.

The maximum number of Shares issued and to be issued upon exercise of share options granted and to be granted
(including both exercised, cancelled and outstanding options) pursuant to the Share Option Scheme and any other
share option schemes to: (A) each participant in the 12-month period up to and including the date of grant, shall not
exceed 1% of the total number of Shares in issue; and (B) a participant of a substantial shareholder or an independent
non-executive director, or any of their respective associates in the 12-month period up to and including the date of
grant (i) representing in aggregate shall not exceed 0.1% of the total number of Shares in issue; and (ii) having an
aggregate value, based on the closing price of the Shares as stated in the Stock Exchange’s daily quotations sheet on
the date of each grant, shall not in excess of HK$5,000,000. Any further grant of share options in excess of the above
limits is subject to Shareholders’ approval in general meeting.

The exercise period of share option granted under the Share Option Scheme was determined by the Board at its
absolute discretion, and shall expire no later than the 10th anniversary of date on which the share option was granted.

There was neither any minimum period for which a share option must be held, nor any performance target which
required to be achieved before the share option could be exercised unless otherwise determined by the Board and
specified at the time of the offer.

The consideration of HK$1 for the acceptance of the grant shall be remitted in favour of the Company within 28 days
from the date of offer or other period as may be determined by the Board.

The exercise price of share options was determined by the Board in its absolute discretion, and shall be at least the
higher of (i) the closing price of the Shares as stated in the daily quotations sheet issued by the Stock Exchange on
the date of offer; and (ii) the average closing price of the Shares as stated in the daily quotations sheets issued by
the Stock Exchange for the five consecutive days on which Shares are traded on the Stock Exchange immediately
preceding the date of offer.
                                                                                                                     Guotai Junan International | Annual Report 2021                   129

                                                                                                                                                    Report of the Directors

Shares). As at the date of this annual report, a total number of Shares available for issue in respect of the share

Share Award Scheme
The Company adopted the Share Award Scheme on 27 October 2011 (as amended on 30 October 2014 and
25 November 2015 subsequently). The Share Award Scheme was valid for a period of 10 years from the date of
adoption and has terminated on 27 October 2021. All awarded Shares became vested upon the termination of the
Share Award Scheme.

The purpose of the Share Award Scheme was (i) to recognise the contributions by certain employees and directors of
any member of the Group and to give incentives in order to retain them for the continuing operation and development
of the Group; and (ii) to attract suitable personnel for further development of the Group.

The maximum number of Shares which can be awarded under the Share Award Scheme was 10% of the Shares in
issue on the adoption date (i.e. 164,000,000 Shares), and the maximum number of the Shares which may be awarded
to a grantee in the 12-month period up to and including the date of award shall not in aggregate exceed 1% of the
Shares in issue on the adoption date (i.e. 16,400,000 Shares).

When a grantee has satisfied all vesting conditions, which might include performance, operating and financial targets
and other criteria, as determined by the Board at the time of making the award and become entitled to the Shares
forming the subject of the award, the trustee shall then transfer respective awarded Shares to that employee or director
at no cost. There is no minimum period for which an awarded Share must be held before it can be vested.

No payment to purchase Shares shall be made, and no instruction to acquire Shares shall be given, to the trustee of
the Share Award Scheme where any director is in possession of inside information in relation to the Company or where
dealing in securities by directors are prohibited under any codes or rules and applicable laws from time to time.

Before the Shares are transferred to the grantee, the voting rights and powers of any Shares held by the trustee shall
be exercised by the trustee who shall abstain from voting.

During the year, 1,000,000 Shares were awarded, 544,000 Shares were lapsed and 9,856,000 Shares were vested. As
at 31 December 2021, no Shares were unvested. Movements of the awarded Shares under the Share Award Scheme
for the year ended 31 December 2021 are set out as follow, details of which are set out in note 32 to the financial

Continuing Connected Transactions
On 2 December 2020, the Company and Guotai Junan Securities Co., Ltd. (“GTJA”) entered into a master agreement
(the “Master Agreement”), pursuant to which both parties conditionally agreed that each of them will, and will
procure the Company (and its subsidiaries from time to time, collectively referred to as the “Group”) and GTJA (and
its subsidiaries from time to time, collectively referred to as “GTJA Group”) respectively, to provide 1) brokerage
services, 2) investment management and advisory services, 3) consulting services, 4) corporate finance services to,
and to engage in 5) investment and finance transactions with, the other for a term of three years from 1 January
2021 to 31 December 2023. As GTJA is the ultimate controlling shareholder of the Company and therefore is a
connected person of the Company, the transactions contemplated under the Master Agreement constitute continuing
connected transactions of the Company and are expected to be of a recurrent nature and may occur on a regular and
continuous basis in the ordinary and usual course of business of the Group. The transactions contemplated under the
Master Agreement are subject to reporting, announcement, annual review and independent Shareholders’ approval
requirement as part of the applicable percentage ratios exceeds 5% under the Listing Rules. The Master Agreement
and the transactions contemplated thereunder have been approved by the independent Shareholders on 20 January
2021. For further details, please refer to the circular dated 5 January 2021.
                                                                          Guotai Junan International | Annual Report 2021   133

                                                                                                   Report of the Directors




The Group and GTJA Group shall, from time to time during the term of the Master Agreement, enter into separate
operational agreements in respect of the provision of any services and transactions, provided that such operational
agreements and their terms shall be subject to and shall not exceed the terms of the Master Agreement.

The annual caps in respect of continuing connected transactions contemplated under the Master Agreement for three
years ended 31 December 2021, and ending 31 December 2022 and 2023 are shown as below:

                                                 For the year ended      For the year ending         For the year ending

Details of the related party transactions undertaken by the Group in the normal course of business are set out in
note 40 to the financial statements of this report, some of those transactions constituted non-exempted continuing
connected transactions under the Listing Rules, for which the Company has complied with applicable disclosure
requirements in accordance with Chapter 14A of the Listing Rules in above.

The Independent Non-executive Directors have reviewed and confirmed that the aforesaid continuing connected
transactions were entered into (i) in the ordinary and usual course of business of the Group; (ii) on normal commercial
terms or better; and (iii) in accordance with the relevant agreements governing them on terms that are fair and
reasonable and in the interests of the Shareholders as a whole.

According to the Group’s policy on pricing and internal control measures for continuing connected transactions,
the relevant business units are required to consult and obtain pre-approvals from the relevant departments for each
continuing connected transaction (except for brokerage transactions with standard brokerage rate applied). Each of
the legal and compliance, risk management, finance departments and office of the board of the Company is assigned
to review each of the continuing connected transactions from their respective expertise with reference to comparable
transactions with independent third parties. The Group regularly monitors transactions between the Group and the
GTJA Group as to whether such transactions fall within the scope of the Master Agreement, whether the relevant
annual caps have nearly been fully utilised, and issues reminders to the responsible business units where necessary.
The Group also conducts sampling inspections based on the internal audit plan so as to ensure the appropriateness
and effectiveness of its internal control procedures.

Report of the Directors




The Directors engaged the auditor of the Company to report on the aforesaid continuing connected transactions in
accordance with Hong Kong Standard on Assurance Engagement 3000 (Revised) “Assurance Engagements Other
Than Audits or Reviews of Historical Financial Information” (“HKSAE 3000”) and with reference to Practice Note 740
“Auditor’s letter on Continuing Connected Transactions under the Hong Kong Listing Rules” (“PN740”) issued by the
Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants.

The auditor has reported to the Directors in accordance with HKSAE 3000 and with reference to PN740 that the
transactions (i) had been approved by the Board; (ii) were in accordance with the pricing policies of the Company;
(iii) had been entered into in accordance with the relevant agreements governing such transactions; and (iv) had not
exceeded the relevant annual caps.


Disclosure Pursuant to Rule 13.21 of the Listing Rules
On 21 August 2020, the Company (as guarantor) and Guotai Junan (Hong Kong) limited (“GJHK”), a wholly-owned
subsidiary of the Company (as borrower), entered into several committed facility agreements with certain banks in Hong
Kong (as lenders) (the “1st Facility Agreement(s)”) in relation to the committed revolving facilities with an aggregate
amount of up to HK$2,150,000,000 with final repayment date falling 36 months after the date of respective 1st Facility
Agreement(s) and an aggregate amount of up to HK$1,350,000,000 with the final repayment date falling 24 months
after the date of respective 1st Facility Agreement(s).

On 25 September 2020, the Company (as guarantor) and GJHK (as borrower) entered into a committed facility
agreement with a bank in Hong Kong (as lender) (the “2nd Facility Agreement”, together with the 1st Facility
Agreement(s) collectively referred to as the “Facility Agreements”) in relation to the committed revolving facility with
an amount of up to HK$500,000,000 with the final repayment date falling 24 months after the date of the 2nd Facility
Agreement.

Under the Facility Agreements, it will be an event of default if Guotai Junan Securities Co., Ltd., the controlling
shareholder of the Company, is not, or ceases (directly or indirectly) to be the single largest legal and beneficial holder
of shares in the Company or does not, or cease to, control the Company. In case of the occurrence of an event of
default, the lender(s) may, by notice to borrower, (i) cancel all or part of the commitment(s); and/or (ii) declare all or
part of the loan(s), together with accrued interest, and all other amounts accrued or outstanding under the Facility
Agreements be immediately due and payable; and/or (iii) declare that all or part of the loan(s) become payable on
demand.


Permitted Indemnity
Subject to the CO and under the Articles of Association, every Director is entitled to be indemnified out of the assets
of the Company against all losses and liabilities which he or she may sustain or incur in or about the execution or
discharge of his or her duties and/or the exercise of his or her powers and/or otherwise in connection with his or
her duties, powers or office. In addition, the Company has maintained appropriate Directors’ and Officers’ Liability
Insurance in respect of relevant legal actions against the directors and officers.


Sufficiency of Public Float
Based on information that is publicly available to the Company and within the knowledge of the Directors, at least 25%
of the total number of issued Shares was held by the public throughout the year ended 31 December 2021 and up to
the date of this report.

Corporate Governance
The Company’s corporate governance principles and practices are set out in the Corporate Governance Report on
pages 104 to 119 of this report.


Compliance with Laws and Regulations
The major laws and regulations that have a significant impact on the Group’s business include the CO, the SFO, the
Anti-Money Laundering and Counter-Terrorist Financing Ordinance (Cap. 615 of the Laws of Hong Kong) and the
Listing Rules. Throughout the year of 2021 and as at the date of this report, as far as the Board and management are
aware, there was no material breach of, or non-compliance with, the applicable laws and regulations by the Group that
has a significant impact on its businesses and operations.


Environmental, Social and Corporate Responsibility
The Company creates sustainable value for its stakeholders by proactively managing risks deriving from environmental,
social and governance conditions. ESG practices are embedded across all operations of the Group and the Board
recognizes the different needs in economic, environmental and social by various stakeholder groups. In achieving ESG-
related goals, the Company has identified certain ESG areas that present challenges and opportunities for its business
to operate in a responsible and sustainable manner. The Company has joined the United Nations Global Compact (“UN
Global Compact”) this year and is committed to supporting the ten principles of the UN Global Compact in areas of
human rights, labour, environment and anti-corruption.

The participation of stakeholders is essential for the development of the Group. The Group attaches its great
importance to the expectations and needs of various stakeholders and has strengthened the existing communication
mechanism. It has also engaged an independent consultant to collect feedback from stakeholders both internally
and externally by way of online survey. For further details about its ESG performance, please refer to the “2021 ESG
Report” to be issued by the Company.


Auditor
The consolidated financial statements of the Group for the year ended 31 December 2021 have been audited by
KPMG, Certified Public Accountants and Public Interest Entity Auditor registered in accordance with the Financial
Reporting Council Ordinance, who will retire, being eligible, and has offered itself for the re-appointment at the AGM to
be held on 31 May 2022.

2.1 Basis of preparation                                                          2.1 編製基準
      These financial statements have been prepared in accordance                   該等財務報表乃根據香港會計師公
      with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) (which                會（「香 港 會 計 師 公 會」）頒 佈 的 香
      include all applicable HKFRSs, Hong Kong Accounting Standards                 港財務報告準則（「香港財務報告準
      (“HKASs”) and Interpretations) issued by the Hong Kong Institute              則」）（其 包 括 所 有 適 用 的 香 港 財 務
      of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”), accounting principles             報 告 準 則、香 港 會 計 準 則（「香 港 會
      generally accepted in Hong Kong and the Hong Kong Companies                   計 準 則」）及 詮 釋）、香 港 公 認 會 計
      Ordinance. They have been prepared under the historical cost                  原 則 及 香 港 公 司 條 例 編 製。除 按 公
      convention, except for financial assets and financial liabilities at fair     平值計入損益的金融資產及金融負
      value through profit or loss, financial assets at fair value through          債、按 公 平 值 計 入 其 他 全 面 收 益 的
      other comprehensive income, debt securities in issue designated at            金 融 資 產、指 定 按 公 平 值 計 入 損 益
      fair value through profit or loss and derivative financial instruments        的已發行債務證券以及衍生金融工
      which have been measured at fair value.                                       具 以 公 平 值 計 量 外，該 等 財 務 報 表
                                                                                    已根據歷史成本慣例編製。

      These financial statements are presented in Hong Kong dollars                 除 非 另 有 說 明，該 等 財 務 報 表 乃 以
      and all values are rounded to the nearest thousand except when                港 元 呈 列，且 所 有 數 值 已 約 整 至 最
      otherwise stated.                                                             接近之千位數。

      Basis of consolidation                                                        綜合基準
      The consolidated financial statements include the financial                  綜合財務報表載有截至2021年12月
      statements of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively                 31日止年度的本公司及其附屬公司
      referred to as the “Group”) for the year ended 31 December 2021.             （下 文 統 稱「本 集 團」）財 務 報 表。附
      A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity), directly or       屬公司為本公司直接或間接控制的
      indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control is achieved when              實體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團
      the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its            承受或享有參與被投資公司業務的
      involvement with the investee and has the ability to affect those            可變回報以及能透過對被投資公司
      returns through its power over the investee (i.e., existing rights that      的 權 力（即 本 集 團 獲 賦 予 現 有 能 力
      give the Group the current ability to direct the relevant activities of      以主導被投資公司相關活動的既存
      the investee).                                                               權 利）影 響 該 等 回 報 時，即 取 得 控
                                                                                   制權。

2.1 Basis of preparation (continued)                                           2.1 編製基準（續）
     When the Company has, directly or indirectly, less than a majority          倘本公司直接或間接擁有被投資公
     of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group considers all     司不足構成大多數的投票權或類似
     relevant facts and circumstances in assessing whether it has power          權 利，則 本 集 團 於 評 估 其 是 否 擁 有
     over an investee, including:                                                對被投資公司的權力時會考慮一切
                                                                                 相關事實及情況，包括：

     (a)   the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders of the        (a)   與被投資公司其他投票權持
           investee;                                                                   有人的合約安排；

     (b)   rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and               (b)   其他合約安排所產生的權利；
                                                                                       及

     (c)   the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.                (c)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

     The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared for               附屬公司的財務報表乃與本公司於
     the same reporting period as the Company, using consistent                  相同申報期間採納一致的會計政策
     accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries are consolidated           編 製。附 屬 公 司 業 績 自 本 集 團 取 得
     from the date on which the Group obtains control, and continue to           控 制 權 當 日 起 綜 合 入 賬，並 將 一 直
     be consolidated until the date that such control ceases.                    綜合入賬直至該控制權終止當日。

     Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive income             損益及其他全面收益的各組成部分
     are attributed to the owners of the parent of the Group and to the          乃屬本集團母公司擁有人及非控股
     non-controlling interests, even if this results in the non-controlling      權 益 所 有，即 使 此 舉 將 導 致 非 控 股
     interests having a deficit balance. All intra-group assets and              權 益 產 生 虧 絀 餘 額。有 關 本 集 團 成
     liabilities, equity, income, expenses and cash flows relating to            員公司間交易之全部集團內公司間
     transactions between members of the Group are eliminated in full            資 產 及 負 債、權 益、收 入、開 支 及
     on consolidation.                                                           現金流量將於綜合入賬時悉數抵銷。

     The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if              本集團將於有事實及情況顯示上述
     facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or           三個控制要素中的一個或多個出現
     more of the three elements of control described above. A change in          變動時重新評估是否控制被投資公
     the ownership interest of a subsidiary (without a loss of control) is       司。附 屬 公 司 之 擁 有 權 變 動（並 無
     accounted for as an equity transaction.                                     失去控制權）將計入股權交易。

     Certain comparative figures have been reclassified to conform               若干比較數字乃經重新分類以與本
     current year’s presentation.                                                年度所呈列者一致。

2.3 Summary of significant accounting policies                                   2.3 主要會計政策概要
     Fair value measurement                                                        公平值計量
     The Group measures its financial assets and financial liabilities at          本集團於各報告期末按公平值計量
     fair value through profit or loss, financial assets at fair value through     其按公平值計入損益的金融資產及
     other comprehensive income, debt securities in issue designated at            金 融 負 債、按 公 平 值 計 入 其 他 全 面
     fair value through profit or loss and derivative financial instruments,       收 益 的 金 融 資 產、指 定 為 按 公 平 值
     fund investments, debt investments and equity investments at fair             計 入 損 益 之 已 發 行 債 務 證 券、衍 生
     value at the end of each reporting period. Fair value is the price that       金 融 工 具、基 金 投 資、債 務 投 資 及
     would be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an      股 本 投 資。公 平 值 為 市 場 參 與 者 於
     orderly transaction between market participants at the measurement            計量日期在有序交易中出售資產所
     date. The fair value measurement is based on the presumption that             收取的價格或轉讓負債所支付的價
     the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place       格。公 平 值 計 量 乃 基 於 出 售 資 產 或
     either in the principal market for the asset or liability, (or in the         轉讓負債的交易於資產或負債主要
     absence of a principal market) in the most advantageous market for            市場或（在無主要市場情況下）最具
     the asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market         優勢的資產或負債市場進行的假設
     must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a              而 作 出。主 要 或 最 具 優 勢 市 場 須 為
     liability is measured using the assumptions that market participants          本 集 團 可 進 入 之 市 場。資 產 或 負 債
     would use when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market           的公平值乃採用市場參與者於資產
     participants act in their economic best interest.                             或 負 債 定 價 時 會 採 用 的 假 設 計 量，
                                                                                   並假定市場參與者以其最佳經濟利
                                                                                   益行事。

     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into                  非金融資產的公平值計量須計及市
     account a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits          場參與者透過最大限度使用該資產
     by using the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to            達 致 最 佳 用 途，或 將 該 資 產 出 售 予
     another market participant that would use the asset in its highest            將最大限度使用該資產達致最佳用
     and best use.                                                                 途 的 其 他 市 場 參 與 者，以 產 生 經 濟
                                                                                   效益的能力。

     The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in                   本集團採納適用於不同情況且具備
     the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to              充分數據以供計量公平值的估值方
     measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable inputs          法，以 盡 量 使 用 相 關 可 觀 察 參 數 及
     and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                                盡量減少使用無法觀察參數。

                             fair value measurement is observable (either                   可 觀 察（直 接 或 間 接）
                             directly or indirectly)                                        的估值方法

                             lowest level input that is significant to the fair             屬重大的最低層參數為
                             value measurement is unobservable                              無法觀察的估值方法

      transfers have occurred between levels in the hierarchy by                    報 告 期 末 重 新 評 估 分 類（基 於 對 公
      reassessing categorisation (based on the lowest level input that is           平值計量整體而言屬重大的最低層
      significant to the fair value measurement as a whole) at the end of           參數）確定是否發生不同等級轉移。
      each reporting period.

      impairment testing for an asset is required (other than financial             對資產（金融資產除外）進行年度減

      recoverable amount is the higher of the asset’s or cash-generating            回 金 額。資 產 的 可 收 回 金 額 為 該 資
      unit’s value in use and its fair value less costs of disposal, and            產或現金產生單位的使用價值與其
      is determined for an individual asset, unless the asset does not              公 平 值 減 出 售 成 本 中 的 較 高 者，並
      generate cash inflows that are largely independent of those from              就 個 別 資 產 予 以 釐 定，除 非 該 資 產
      other assets or groups of assets, in which case the recoverable               並不產生很大程度上獨立於其他資
      amount is determined for the cash-generating unit to which the                產 或 資 產 組 別 的 現 金 流 入，在 此 情
      asset belongs.                                                                況 下，可 收 回 金 額 乃 就 該 資 產 所 屬
                                                                                    現金產生單位予以釐定。

     amount that would have been determined (net of any depreciation)          無就資產確認減值虧損而應已釐定

     loss in the period in which it arises.

            In calculating the present value of lease payments, the                在計算租賃付款現值時，由於
            Group uses its incremental borrowing rate at the lease                 租賃隱含的利率無法立即確

            lease is not readily determinable. After the commencement              的 遞 增 借 貸 利 率。開 始 日 期
            date, the amount of lease liabilities is increased to reflect          後，增加租賃負債金額以反映

                Stage 3: Lifetime ECL – credit-impaired                                   第 三 階 段：全 期 預 期
                                                                                          信用損失－信貸減值
               Exposures are assessed as credit-impaired when one                      當發生會對該資產估計
               or more events that have a detrimental impact on the                    未來現金流量造成不利
               estimated future cash flows of that asset have occurred.                影響的一項或多項事件，
               For exposures that have become credit-impaired,                         則有關情況會被評定為
               a lifetime ECL is recognised and interest revenue is                    出 現 信 貸 減 值。就 已 出
               calculated by applying the effective interest rate to the               現信貸減值的情況而言，
               amortised cost (net of provision) rather than the gross                 會確認全期預期信用損
               carrying amount.                                                        失，並 透 過 於 攤 銷 成 本
                                                                                       （扣 除 撥 備）而 非 賬 面 總
                                                                                       值 應 用 實 際 利 率，以 計
                                                                                       算利息收入。

               At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                       於 各 報 告 日 期，本 集 團
               there has been a significant increase in credit risk for                 會將由報告日期至初步
               exposures since initial recognition by comparing the                     確認日期的預計生命周
               risk of default occurring over the expected life between                 期內發生的違約風險比
               the reporting date and the date of initial recognition.                  較，以 評 估 自 初 步 確 認
               The Group considers reasonable and supportable                           以來信貸風險是否顯著
               information that is relevant and available without undue                 增 加。本 集 團 就 此 會 考
               cost or effort for this purpose. This includes quantitative              慮相關及毋須支付過多
               and qualitative information and also, forward-looking                    成本或努力而已可得的
               analysis. For certain portfolio of margin loans, the Group               合理及具支持理據的資
               rebuts the presumption that there have been significant                  料，當 中 包 括 量 化 及 質
               increases in credit risk since initial recognition when                  化資料以及前瞻性分析。
               financial assets are more than 30 days past due as                       關於若干孖展貸款組合，
               management considers the probability of default is highly                當 金 融 資 產 逾 期 超 過30
               correlated with the collateral value rather than the past                天 時，由 於 管 理 層 認 為
               due days.                                                                違約機會與抵押價值（而
                                                                                        非 逾 期 天 數）具 密 切 關
                                                                                        連，故 本 集 團 將 自 初 步
                                                                                        確認以來信貸風險顯著

     Derivative financial instruments                                            衍生金融工具
     The Group’s derivative financial instruments are initially recognised       本集團衍生金融工具於衍生合約訂
     at their fair value on the date on which a derivative contract is           立 日 期 初 步 按 其 公 平 值 確 認，其 後
     entered into and are subsequently remeasured at fair value.                 按公平值再次計量。

     Derivatives are carried as assets when the fair value is positive and       倘 公 平 值 為 正 數，衍 生 工 具 以 資 產
     as liabilities when the fair value is negative. Any gains or losses         入 賬，而 公 平 值 倘 為 負 數，則 以 負
     arising from changes in fair value of derivatives are taken directly to     債 入 賬。衍 生 工 具 的 公 平 值 變 動 產
     statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income.                 生的任何盈虧直接計入損益及其他
                                                                                 全面收益表。

     Whilst the Group enters into a derivative contract for trading              當本集團根據本集團風險管理框架
     purposes or to provide economic hedges under the Group’s risk               為交易目的或提供經濟對沖而訂立
     management framework, it does not apply hedge accounting.                   衍 生 工 具 合 約 時，並 不 應 用 對 沖 會
                                                                                 計。

     Offsetting of financial instruments                                         抵銷金融工具
     Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the net           倘存在現時可強制執行的法律權利
     amount is reported in the statement of financial position if there is       可 抵 銷 已 確 認 金 額，且 有 意 以 淨 額
     a currently enforceable legal right to offset the recognised amounts        基準結算或同時變現資產及清償
     and there is an intention to settle on a net basis, or to realise the       負 債，則 可 抵 銷 金 融 資 產 及 金 融 負
     assets and settle the liabilities simultaneously.                           債，而淨額於財務狀況表呈報。

     Shares held under the share award scheme                                    股份獎勵計劃項下持有的
                                                                                 股份
     Own equity instruments which are reacquired (shares held under              購 回 本 身 的 權 益 工 具（股 份 獎 勵 計
     the share award scheme) are recognised at cost and deducted                 劃 項 下 持 有 的 股 份）按 成 本 確 認 並
     from equity. No gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss on the         於 權 益 中 扣 除。本 集 團 購 買、出
     purchase, sale, issue or cancellation of the Group’s own equity             售、發 行 或 註 銷 本 身 的 權 益 工 具 所
     instruments. Any difference between the carrying amount and the             得 收 益 或 虧 損 概 不 於 損 益 賬 確 認。
     consideration is recognised in equity.                                      賬面值與代價之間的任何差額於權
                                                                                 益確認。

      Client trust bank balances                                                    客戶信託銀行結餘
      The Group maintains trust and segregated accounts with authorised             本集團於獲授權金融機構開設信託
      financial institutions to hold clients’ deposits arising from normal          及 獨 立 賬 戶，以 保 管 客 戶 來 自 一 般
      business transactions. The Group has classified the clients’ monies           業 務 交 易 的 存 款。本 集 團 將 客 戶 款
      as client trust bank balances under the current assets in the                 項歸類為綜合財務狀況表流動資產
      consolidated statement of financial position as the Group is allowed          項 下 的 客 戶 信 託 銀 行 結 餘，原 因 為
      to retain some or all of the interest income on the clients’ monies           本集團獲准保留客戶款項的部分或
      and recognised corresponding accounts payable to the respective               全 部 利 息 收 入，並 基 於 其 對 客 戶 款
      customers in the current liabilities clients on grounds that it is liable     項的任何損失或挪用負責而確認應
      for any loss or misappropriation of clients’ monies. Under the Hong           付流動負債賬戶內有關客戶的相應
      Kong Securities and Futures Ordinance (Cap. 571) and the Hong                 款 項。根 據 香 港 法 例 第571章 香 港
      Kong Insurance Companies Ordinance (Cap. 41), the Group is not                證券及期貨條例及香港法例第41章
      allowed to use the clients’ monies to settle its own obligation.              香 港 保 險 公 司 條 例，本 集 團 不 得 動
                                                                                    用客戶款項清償其自身債務。

      Fiduciary activities                                                          受託活動
      Apart from the client trust bank balances as mentioned above, the             除上文所述的客戶信託銀行結餘

      Group acts in a fiduciary capacity which results in the holding or            理 服 務，本 集 團 在 當 中 以 受 託 人 身
      placing of assets on behalf of its customers. These assets and any            份 行 事，以 致 須 代 其 客 戶 持 有 或 配
      gains or losses arising thereon are not included in these financial           售 資 產。該 等 資 產 及 其 所 產 生 的 任
      statements as the Group has no contractual rights to these assets             何收益或虧損不會計入該等財務報
      and its gains or losses under fiduciary activities.                           表，原 因 為 本 集 團 對 該 等 資 產 及 其
                                                                                    根據受託活動的收益或虧損概無合

      Cash and cash equivalents                                                     現金及現金等價物
      For the purpose of the consolidated statement of cash flows, cash             就 綜 合 現 金 流 量 表 而 言，現 金 及 現

      acquired, less bank overdrafts which are repayable on demand and              短 期 限 到 期，減 須 按 要 求 償 還 的 銀
      form an integral part of the Group’s cash management.                         行 透 支，並 構 成 本 集 團 現 金 管 理 的
                                                                                    一部分。

      For the purpose of the consolidated statement of financial position,          就 綜 合 財 務 狀 況 表 而 言，現 金 及 現
      cash and cash equivalents comprise cash on hand and at banks,                 金 等 價 物 包 括 手 頭 及 銀 行 現 金（包
      including term deposits, and assets similar in nature to cash, which          括定期存款及與現金性質相似的資
      are not restricted as to use.                                                 產），其用途並無限制。

     Provisions                                                                 撥備

     the obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can be made of the       並 能 夠 可 靠 估 計 責 任 金 額 時，則 確
     amount of the obligation.                                                  認撥備。

     the obligation. The increase in the discounted present value amount        導 致 折 現 現 值 的 金 額 增 加，會 列 入
     arising from the passage of time is included in finance costs in           損益賬的融資成本。
     profit or loss.

           The dilutive effect (if any) of outstanding options is reflected           尚未行使購股權的攤薄影響
           as additional share dilution in the computation of earnings per            （如 有）乃 於 計 算 每 股 盈 利 時
           share.                                                                     反映為額外股份攤薄。

     Dividends                                                                  股息
     Final dividends are recognised as a liability when they are approved       末期股息將於股東大會上獲股東批
     by the shareholders in a general meeting.                                  准後確認為負債。

     Interim dividends are simultaneously proposed and declared,                由於本公司組織章程細則授予董事
     because the Company’s articles of association grant the directors          會 權 力 宣 派 中 期 股 息，擬 派 中 期 股
     the authority to declare interim dividends. Consequently, interim          息 可 即 時 宣 派。因 此，中 期 股 息 於
     dividends are recognised immediately as a liability when they are          擬派發及宣派時即時確認為負債。
     proposed and declared.

      Loans and advances are categorised into “excellent”, “good”, “fair”         貸 款 及 墊 款 分 類 為「 優 秀 」、「 良
      and “individually impaired”. Excellent refers to exposures which            好」、「普 通」及「個 別 減 值」。優 秀
      margin obligation are consistently met and the payment of principal         指貫徹履行孖展責任及本金和利息
      and interest is not in doubt, collaterals provided are highly liquid        付 款 並 無 呆 賬，且 提 供 的 抵 押 品 具
      and of good quality. Good refers to exposures which principal               有 高 流 通 性 及 良 好 品 質 的 風 險。良
      and interest are fully secured but collaterals provided are at a            好指本金和利息全部有抵押但提供
      relatively lower quality where shortfall may be recorded when there         的 抵 押 品 質 量 相 對 較 低，且 抵 押 品
      is a significant drop in collateral value. Fair refers to exposures         價值大幅下跌時可能錄得缺額的風
      which principal and interest are fully secured but the quality of           險。普 通 指 本 金 和 利 息 全 部 有 抵 押
      the collateral is deteriorating, shortfall would be expected when           但 抵 押 品 質 量 正 在 變 差，且 抵 押 品
      the collateral value continues to deteriorate. Individually impaired        價值持續下跌時預期錄得缺額的風
      refers to exposures which loss, partial or full, has incurred and with      險。個 別 減 值 指 已 產 生 部 分 或 悉 數
      insufficient collateral.                                                    虧損且無足夠抵押品的風險。

                                                                               （續）

     Margin financing operations                                                孖展融資業務
     The Group provides customers with margin financing for securities          本集團就證券交易向客戶提供孖展
     transactions, which are secured by customers’ securities held as           融 資，以 客 戶 的 證 券 持 作 抵 押 品 擔
     collateral. The maximum credit limit granted for each customer is          保。每 名 客 戶 獲 授 的 最 高 信 貸 額 度
     based on the customer’s financial background and the quality of            乃以客戶的財務背景及所持相關抵
     related collateral. The Group seeks to maintain strict control over        押 品 的 質 素 為 基 準。本 集 團 擬 保 持
     its outstanding receivables and has a credit and risk management           對 其 未 清 償 應 收 款 項 的 嚴 格 控 制，
     department to monitor credit risks.                                        並成立了信貸及風險管理部門監控
                                                                                信貸風險。

     Margin loans to the customers are secured by the underlying                給予客戶的孖展貸款以相關已抵押
     pledged securities, bear interest at a rate with reference to the          證 券 擔 保，按 根 據 港 元 最 優 惠 利 率
     Hong Kong dollar prime rate and are repayable on demand. The               釐 定 的 利 率 計 息，並 按 要 求 償 還。
     carrying value of margin loans approximates to their fair value. No        孖展貸款的賬面值與其公平值相
     ageing analysis is disclosed as, in the opinion of the directors, an       若。由 於 董 事 認 為，鑒 於 孖 展 貸 款
     ageing analysis does not give additional value in view of the nature       業 務 的 性 質，賬 齡 分 析 並 不 會 提 供
     of the margin loans business. The amount of credit facilities granted      額 外 價 值，故 概 無 披 露 賬 齡 分 析。
     to margin clients is determined by the discounted market value of          授予孖展客戶的信貸融資額乃根據
     the collateral securities accepted by the Group. As at 31 December         本集團接納的抵押證券貼現市值釐
     2021, the total value of securities pledged as collateral in respect       定。於2021年12月31日，作 為 孖 展
     of the margin loans was approximately HK$58,946 million (2020:             貸款抵押品的已抵押證券總值約為
     HK$48,183 million) based on the market value of the securities as          58,946百 萬 港 元（ 2020年：48,183
     at the end of the reporting period.                                        百 萬 港 元），此 乃 按 證 券 於 報 告 期
                                                                                末的市值計算得出。

     Term loans to customers                                                    給予客戶的定期貸款

     the interest rates, reference is made to the credit standing of            釐 定 利 率 時，會 參 考 相 關 客 戶 的 財
     the relevant customers and the quality and value of the collateral         務信貸狀況以及所質押的抵押品質
     pledged. The term loans granted to customers amounted to                   素 及 價 值。於2021年12月31日 授 予

     customers approximate to their fair values.                               （ 2020年：32,290,000港 元）。給 予

    agreements
     The receivable from reverse repurchase agreements arises                  反向回購協議之應收款項於本集團
     when the securities are bought by the Group with a concurrent             購 買 證 券 時 產 生，連 帶 同 時 訂 立 一
     agreement to resell at a specified later date and price. These            項協議以按指定其後日期及價格
     securities are not recognised in the Group’s consolidated statement       轉 售。該 等 證 券 並 無 於 本 集 團 綜 合
     of financial position as the counterparty retains substantially           財 務 狀 況 表 中 確 認，原 因 是 交 易 方
     all risks and returns of the securities. The amount paid by the           保留該等證券的絕大部分風險及回
     Group is recognised as receivable. In the event of default by             報。本 集 團 支 付 的 款 項 確 認 為 應 收
     the counterparty, the Group has the right to sell the underlying          款 項。倘 交 易 方 違 約，本 集 團 有 權
     securities for settling the outstanding receivable.                       出售相關證券以結算未結清的應收
                                                                               款項。

29. Obligations under repurchase agreements                                                          29. 回購協議的債項
      The obligations under repurchase agreements arise when the                                        本 集 團 根 據 回 購 協 議 出 售 證 券，連
      securities are sold by the Group with a concurrent agreement to                                   帶同時訂立一項協議以按指定其後
      repurchase at a specified later date and price. The amount received                               日 期 及 價 格 回 購 證 券。本 集 團 收 取
      by the Group is recognised as liabilities as the Group retains                                    的 款 項 確 認 為 負 債，原 因 是 本 集 團
      substantially all risks and returns of the securities.                                            保留證券的絕大部分風險及回報。

38. Unconsolidated structured entity                                          38. 未綜合結構性實體
      A structured entity is an entity in which voting rights are not the       結構性實體為投票權並非決定控制
      dominant factor in deciding control. Structured entity is generally       權 的 主 導 因 素 的 實 體。結 構 性 實 體
      created to achieve a narrow and well defined objective with               通常為實現有限而明確的目標而
      restrictions around their ongoing activities.                             設，其持續經營活動受到限制。

      Depending on the Group’s power over the activities of the entity          視乎本集團對實體活動的權力及其
      and its exposure to and the ability to influence its own returns, it      受實體回報的影響程度及其影響實
      may consolidate the entity. In other cases, it may sponsor or have        體 回 報 的 能 力 而 定，其 可 能 將 實 體
      an interest in such an entity but does not consolidate it. The Group      綜 合 入 賬。在 其 他 情 況 下，其 可 能
      sponsors an entity when:                                                  資 助 實 體 或 於 實 體 中 擁 有 權 益，但
                                                                                不 將 實 體 綜 合 入 賬。下 列 情 況 屬 本
                                                                                集團贊助一間實體：

      (i)     It is the majority user of the entity;                            (i)     其為該實體的主要用戶；

      (ii)    Its name appears in the name of the entity or on the products     (ii)    其名稱出現在該實體的名稱
              issued by the entity;                                                     或該實體發行的產品上；

      (iii)   It provides implicit or explicit guarantees of the entity’s       (iii)   其為該實體的業績提供隱含
              performance; and                                                          或明確的擔保；及

      (iv)    It led the formation of the entity.                               (iv)    其牽頭成立該實體。

      The unconsolidated structured entities invest in a range of asset         未綜合結構性實體投資於多種資產
      classes. The Group receives income from these unconsolidated              類 別。本 集 團 透 過 自 該 等 實 體 發 行
      structured entities through the receipt of arrangement fees and           的計息票據收取安排費用及利息收
      interest income from interest bearing notes issued by these entities.     入而自該等未綜合結構性實體收取
      As of 31 December 2021, the Group held notes of HK$ 2,519                 收 入。截 至2021年12月31日，本 集
      million (2020: HK$4,536 million), under financial assets held for         團 在 持 作 買 賣 及 投 資 金 融 資 產（附
      trading and investments (note 20), issued by these entities.              註20 ）項 下 持 有 該 等 實 體 發 行 的 票
                                                                                據2,519百 萬 港 元（ 2020年：4,536
                                                                                百萬港元）。

41. Financial risk management                                                    41. 金融風險管理
     The Group’s activities mainly expose to such financial risks as               本 集 團 的 業 務 主 要 面 臨 信 貸 風 險、
     credit risk, market risk and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk         市場風險及流動資金風險等金融
     management framework focuses on the analysis, evaluation,                     風 險。本 集 團 整 體 風 險 管 理 框 架 集
     acceptance and monitoring of these risks which are an inevitable              中 於 分 析、評 估、接 受 及 監 控 該 等
     consequence of being in business. The Group’s aim is therefore                於 業 務 過 程 中 不 可 避 免 的 風 險。因
     to achieve an appropriate balance between risk and return and                 此，本 集 團 的 目 標 為 適 當 地 平 衡 風
     minimise any potential adverse effect on the Group’s financial                險 與 回 報，並 將 對 本 集 團 財 務 表 現
     performance.                                                                  潛在的任何不良影響減至最低。

     The Group’s risk management policies are approved by the board                本集團的風險管理政策由董事會批
     of directors and are designed to identify and analyse these risks, to         准，旨 在 識 別 及 分 析 上 述 風 險，設
     set appropriate risk limits and controls and to monitor the risks and         定適當的風險限額並控制及監控風
     adherence to limits by means of reliable and up-to-date information.          險及使用可靠及先進的資訊以嚴守
     The board of directors provides written principles for overall risk           限 額。董 事 會 就 整 體 風 險 管 理 制 定
     management. The Group regularly reviews its risk management                   書 面 原 則。本 集 團 定 期 檢 討 其 風 險
     policies to reflect any change in the market and clients.                     管 理 政 策，以 反 映 市 場 及 客 戶 的 任
                                                                                   何變動。

     The main financial risks arising from the Group’s financial                   本集團金融工具所產生的主要金融
     instruments are credit risk, market risk and liquidity risk. The              風 險 為 信 貸 風 險、市 場 風 險 及 流 動
     policies for managing each of these risks are summarised below:               資 金 風 險。管 理 各 項 風 險 的 政 策 概
                                                                                   括如下：

     Credit risk                                                                   信貸風險
     Credit exposures arise principally from loans and advances to                 信貸風險主要源自本集團資產組合
     customers, accounts receivable, debt securities, bank balances and            中 的 給 予 客 戶 的 貸 款 及 墊 款、應 收
     client trust bank balances which are included in the Group’s asset            款 項、債 務 證 券、銀 行 結 餘 及 客 戶
     portfolio.                                                                    信託銀行結餘。

     Credit risk limit control and mitigation policies                             信用風險限額控制及緩釋政策
     The board of directors delegates to the management committee,                 董 事 會 委 派 管 理 委 員 會（包 括 信 貸
     include credit committee and risk management committee the                    委 員 會 及 風 險 管 理 委 員 會）監 控 本
     duties to monitor the credit risk of the Group. Members of the                集 團 信 貸 風 險。信 貸 委 員 會 及 風
     credit committee and risk management committee include, inter                 險 管 理 委 員 會 包 括（除 其 他 外）行
     alia, the chief executive officer, the chief financial officer, the chief     政 總 裁、首 席 財 務 總 監、首 席 風 險
     risk officer, responsible officers and the head of related control            總 監、負 責 人 員 及 相 關 監 控 部 門 主
     departments. The credit committee is mainly responsible for the               管。信 貸 委 員 會 主 要 負 責 發 展 孖 展
     development of margin financing business and approval of major                融資業務及批准主要信貸風險及風
     credit exposure and the risk management committee to provide                  險管理委員會獨立監察所有信貸風

41. Financial risk management (continued)                                       41. 金融風險管理（續）
      Market risk                                                                 市場風險
      The Group takes on exposure to market risk, which is the risk               本 集 團 所 面 對 的 市 場 風 險，乃 指 由
      that the fair value or future cash flows of a financial instrument will     於市場價格變動而引致金融工具的
      fluctuate because of changes in market prices. Market risks mainly          公平值或未來現金流量變動的風
      arise from open positions in interest rate, currency and equity             險。市 場 風 險 乃 主 要 因 利 率、貨 幣
      products, all of which are exposed to general and specific market           及 股 權 產 品 等 的 未 平 倉 而 產 生，並
      movements and changes in the level of volatility of market rates or         受到一般及特定的市場變動及市場
      prices such as interest rates, foreign exchange rates and equity            利 率 或 價 格（如 利 率、匯 率 及 股 價）
      prices.                                                                     波幅變動的風險所影響。

      The financial instruments held by the Group mainly exposed                  本集團所持有的金融工具主要面對
      it to interest rate risk and price risk. The market risk mainly             利 率 風 險 及 價 格 風 險。市 場 風 險 主

     Financial instruments measured at fair value                              按公平值計量的金融工具
     Group’s valuation process                                                 本集團的估值流程
     The fair values of financial assets in Level 1 and Level 2 are            第1級 及 第2級 金 融 資 產 的 公 平 值 乃
     measured by reference to quoted market prices and brokers’                分別參考市場報價及經紀報價計
     quotes, respectively. The Group engaged independent professional          量。本 集 團 已 委 聘 獨 立 專 業 估 值 師
     valuers to perform the valuation of financial instruments in Level 3      對 第3級 金 融 工 具 進 行 估 值，以 作
     for financial reporting purposes. These valuation reports are sent to     財 務 申 報 目 的。該 等 估 值 報 告 將 每
     the finance department and the Audit Committee for discussion at          年 至 少 兩 次（與 本 集 團 的 報 告 日 期
     least twice per annum, which is in line with the Group’s reporting        一致）呈送予財務部及審核委員會，
     dates.                                                                    以供討論。

     At each financial year end, the finance department reviews all            於 各 財 政 年 度 末，財 務 部 審 閱 所 有
     significant unobservable inputs and valuation adjustments used to         用 於 計 量 第3級 金 融 工 具 公 平 值 的
     measure the fair values of financial instruments in Level 3. Changes      重 大 無 法 觀 察 參 數 及 估 值 調 整。第
     in fair values of financial assets in Levels 2 and 3 are analysed at      2級及第3級金融資產的公平值變動

      (a) Financial instruments in Level 1                                        (a) 第1級金融工具
            The fair values of financial instruments traded in active                   於活躍市場買賣的金融工具
            markets are based on quoted market prices at the end of                     公平值根據報告期末的市場
            the reporting period. A market is regarded as active if quoted              報價計算。活躍的市場指可即
            prices are readily and regularly available from an exchange,                時及定期從交易所、交易商、
            dealer, or broker and those prices represent actual and                     或經紀取得報價的市場，而該
            regularly occurring market transactions on an arm’s length                  等報價反映按公平基準進行
            basis. The quoted market prices used for financial assets and               的實際及定期發生的市場交
            financial liabilities held by the Group are the current bid price           易。本集團所持金融資產及金
            and the current ask price.                                                  融負債所用的市場報價為當
                                                                                        時的買盤價及賣盤價。

      (b)   Financial instruments in Level 2                                      (b)   第2級金融工具
            The fair values of financial instruments that are not traded                並 非 於 活 躍 市 場（例 如 場 外 市
            in an active market (for example, over-the-counter) are                     場）買 賣 的 金 融 工 具 公 平 值 乃
            determined by using valuation techniques. These valuation                   使用估值技術釐定。該等估值
            techniques maximise the use of observable market data where                 技術盡可能採用可觀察市場
            it is available and rely as little as possible on entity specific           數 據（如 有），盡 量 不 依 賴 實
            estimates. If all significant inputs used in the valuation for              體的特定估計。如金融工具估
            financial instruments are observable, the financial instruments             值所用的所有重大參數均為
            are included in Level 2.                                                    可觀察參數，該金融工具列入
                                                                                        第2級。

            If one or more of the significant inputs are not based on                   倘有一個或多個重大參數並
            observable market data, the financial instrument is included in             非以可觀察市場數據為基準，
            Level 3.                                                                    該金融工具列入第3級。

     Capital management                                                      資本管理
     The Group’s objectives for managing capital, which represent a          本集團資本管理的目標概念比財務
     broader concept than the “equity” on the face of the statement of       狀況表的「權益」更為廣闊，即：
     financial position, are:

     •    to comply with the capital requirements under the Hong Kong        •   就本身為持牌法團的附屬公
          Securities and Futures (Financial Resources) Rules for its             司而言，符合香港證券及期貨
          subsidiaries which are licensed corporations;                          （財政資源）規則的資本規定；

     •    to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going            •   保障本集團持續經營的能力，
          concern so that it can continue to provide returns for                 以繼續為股東提供回報及為
          shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders;                      其他持份者提供利益；

     •    to support the Group’s stability and growth; and                   •   維持本集團的穩定及發展；及

     •    to maintain a strong capital base to support the development       •   維持穩固的資本基礎，以支持
          of its business.                                                       其業務的發展。

     The liquid capital is monitored daily by each individual licensed       各持牌附屬公司的管理層根據證券
     subsidiary’s management based on the Securities and Futures             及期貨（財政資源）規則每天監控流
     (Financial Resources) Rules. The required information is filed          動 資 金，並 按 時 向 證 監 會 提 呈 所 需
     with the SFC on a timely basis. The SFC requires each licensed          資 料。證 監 會 規 定，各 持 牌 法 團 須
     corporation to maintain a level of required liquid capital which is     保持所需流動資本水平為介乎0.1百
     the higher of the floor requirement ranging from HK$0.1 million to      萬港元至15百萬港元的最低要求及
     HK$15 million and 5% of the aggregate of its adjusted liabilities       其經調整負債及客戶保證金總和的
     and clients’ margin. All licensed corporations within the Group         5%兩 者 中 的 較 高 者。於 截 至2021
